F. PRIORITIES FOR TODAY
Prioritize raising the bar for 2026-07-29 where compset median is ฿9,271 versus our ฿6,100, followed by adjustments on 2026-07-26 through 2026-07-28 where rates sit below market. Consider reducing the bar or running a targeted OTA promo for 2026-08-05 where our rate sits above the compset median. Launch a demand-generation campaign for September 2026 to address the sharp year-over-year decline, and execute a similar campaign for August 2026 to recover from the year-over-year drop. The overall stance is to defend rate integrity on near-term dates while pushing volume through campaigns in the coming months.
